# Known answers for tiny-tracker 0.4.0

Generated by `expected/generate.mjs` from running each build; do not edit by hand. Each cell is the set of answers a correct harness may give.
Contract answers are the lost-response driver's own verdict strings at 92bf1ba; LRT-A3 is self-attested until the driver compares recovered digests.
